- **openapi-sync**: Enforces OpenAPI spec discipline when working on REST API endpoints in this project. Triggers whenever adding, modifying, or removing HTTP routes, request/response types, or API handlers in the Rust web-ui crate (`crates/web-ui`). Reminds the agent to (1) update the committed `openapi.json` spec, (2) run `make dump-openapi` to re-export the spec from the running server, and (3) run `make generate-flutter-client` to regenerate the Dart/dio client in `app/packages/assistant_api/`. Also applies when changing route parameters, status codes, or authentication on existing endpoints. ([cedricziel/assistant](https://github.com/cedricziel/assistant/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/openapi-sync) · ★6)
